I find myself hesitating for a long time over things that used to be effortless, which seems to go hand-in-hand with my concentration issues.\n\nDoctor: Have you noticed any changes in your sleep patterns, such as difficulty falling asleep or sleeping too much, or any significant changes in your appetite during this year?\n\nPatient: I haven't really noticed any major changes in my sleep or my appetite. I seem to sleep and eat about the same as I always have, so I'm not sure if those have been affected.\n\nDoctor: Have you noticed a clear loss of interest or pleasure in activities that you used to enjoy, or do you still find yourself engaging in and enjoying your hobbies as you did before?\n\nPatient: To be honest, I haven't really thought about my hobbies or activities lately. I've just been feeling so generally empty, numb, and down that I haven't paid much attention to them.\n\nDoctor: If you were to try one of those hobbies today, do you think you would still feel that same sense of enjoyment you used to get from it, or does the idea of it feel pointless or unappealing now?\n\nPatient: It feels completely pointless and unappealing to even think about doing them now.
